Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The microwave variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 800 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 236 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on microwave structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 800. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 236. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any microwave module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 70. The projective resolution has length at most 603 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.
